> ★★★★★★ **A SEND to a pane with an OPEN /rewind PICKER DISMISSES the picker → the rewind FAILS to land (2026-07-30, po fired a build-go into 0.1 mid-picker = dismissed ARON's picker, expert stayed 59%).** "busy-agent-dismisses-own-picker": ANY send (message, build-go, even MY /context) to a pane while its rewind picker is open cancels the picker. Only /context on a CLEAN composer (or a trivial/recoverable draft I flag to po). Measuring must never eat a real dispatch.
> ★★★★★ **BARE-SHELL PANE = maybe MID-RELAUNCH (Claude booting), NOT necessarily down — RE-CAPTURE before flagging 'agent down/restart it' (2026-07-29, I prematurely flagged planner 0.6 'Claude down' → re-capture one beat later showed it UP with its board-draft).** A pane showing "finding completions / sourcing bash-completion / ❯" is the pre-Claude shell init that runs WHILE claude is booting — it looks like a dead bash shell but the TUI appears seconds later. Wait + re-capture (or check for a recent commit = it was up) before calling it down. Genuinely-down = shell STATIC across multiple captures + no TUI + no relaunch. (Distinct from an Option-2 rewind, which keeps the process alive — a bare-shell = a process restart/relaunch, transient or crash.)
> ★★★★★★ **A LINGERING '0%'/'Context low' BANNER ≠ a live WALL if the pane is ACTIVELY GENERATING (po 2026-07-30, my false-positive on tester 0.5).** The banner can be STALE — it doesn't always clear on an Option-2 rewind recovery. To classify a wall, check BOTH banner AND activity: (a) banner + ACTIVELY GENERATING ("Simmering/Cogitating…" with token count CLIMBING, esc-to-interrupt + tokens flowing) = RECOVERED, stale banner, NOT walled — an agent at true-0% CANNOT generate thousands of tokens. (b) banner + CANNOT generate ("Worked for 0s", 0 tokens, stuck/idle) = REAL wall. **Verify by ACTIVITY (is it generating?), not the banner alone** (same class as context.read-lies: the reading/banner can lie, the generation-signature is truth). RECONCILES with the walled-looks-idle rule below: real wall = banner + no-generation; false alarm = banner + generation. When unsure, watch one beat for the token count to move. ⚠ EXTENDS to SCROLLBACK ERRORS too (2026-07-30, my 2nd false-pos: 'API Error: Server is temporarily limiting requests · Rate limited' lingered in req's scrollback AFTER a ONE-TIME momentary limit → I text-matched it as 'still rate-limited' for 2 ticks; it was STALE). GENERAL RULE: a banner/error TEXT in the pane ≠ a LIVE condition — it can be stale scrollback. TELL: if the state line is IDENTICAL across ticks ('Crunched for 29s' unchanged = frozen/stale display, not live). VERIFY the LIVE state (re-measure: /context responds? generating? activity?) before flagging — never text-match old scrollback. (Applies to: 0%-wall banners, rate-limit errors, any lingering error text.) ⚠ REFINEMENT (po 2026-07-30): "generating = recovered ≠ SAFE" — a recovered agent can STILL be genuinely NEAR-WALL + climbing (tester was ~65%-floor [walled-rewind is ≤~50-65% capped, can't go deeper] + climbing in a heavy gate → the '0%' banner was BOTH lingering AND real-near-wall). So banner+generating: report as "recovered but VERIFY %/watch for RE-WALL" (not "safe"). Confirm real % via /context when it idles; watch for a re-wall mid-heavy-work (banner returns + generation STOPS = re-walled → report to po).
> ★★★★★★ **SWEEP-GAP: a WALLED agent LOOKS IDLE (Tron/po 2026-07-29, my miss on req 0.4).** `hiveMind team.sweep` classifies a walled agent (0% / "Context limit reached" / "Context low (0% remaining)") as IDLE — empty composer + "auto mode on" footer = looks idle, and the stale ⚠% never shows 100%. The wall only surfaces when it can't process a send ("Worked for 0s"). **FIX: read the pane BANNER (the top/status line, not just composer) — the "Context limit reached / N% remaining" text = WALLED. Before reporting any idle agent as "available-to-dispatch" to po, a walled one is NOT dispatchable.** Practically: spot-check high-stale idle agents' pane banners periodically (can't cheaply banner-read every pane every tick, but rotate); and any agent that stays "idle" while it should be working, or shows "Worked for 0s", is a wall suspect → read its banner → if walled, that's a near-wall FLAG to po (they drive the rewind).
> ★★★★★★★ **A BLANK/NO-FOOTER PANE CAN BE A COLLAPSED (h=1) LIVE AGENT — check HEIGHT + un-collapse BEFORE concluding down (po/hiveMind-expert 2026-07-31 planner root).** A live Claude agent CANNOT render its TUI/footer in a 1-row pane → looks blank/dead. The planner had BOTH: my -S scrollback artifact AND an h=1 collapse. **DOWN/WEDGE CHECKLIST (all 3): (1) `tmux list-panes -F '#{pane_index} #{pane_width}x#{pane_height}'` — if h≤~3, it's COLLAPSED not dead → `otmux pane.size.set <pane> <w> <h>` to un-collapse, THEN re-read; (2) `tmux capture-pane -t <pane> -p` (VISIBLE, no -S); (3) `ps` for 'claude --name <role>' (alive/fg/zombie). Only DEAD pid = truly down.** Recovery of a live-wedged/collapsed agent = un-collapse + nudge (NOT kill/relaunch, which destroys state). Root of the bootstrap→ooshShells mistarget = poisoned active.team (dead __test_hm session) — fixed via `hiveMind team.switch robbinTeam2`.
> ★★★★★★★★ **DOWN-DETECTION — `otmux pane.capture` READS SCROLLBACK (-S) AND LIES ON A WEDGED TUI (hiveMind-expert ground-truth 2026-07-31; RETRACTS my earlier 'sustained bash-shell captures = DOWN' — sustained captures of the WRONG TOOL are STILL WRONG).** A pane showing a BASH SHELL ('finding completions / sourcing bash-completion / bare ❯', no Claude footer) is often a TOOL ARTIFACT: on a WEDGED alt-screen Claude TUI, `pane.capture <pane> N` returns STALE 32-day-old bash-startup scrollback, NOT the live screen. planner 0.6 looked shell-DOWN in 3 sustained captures but its PID (claude --name robbin-planner, 51742) was ALIVE+foreground = WEDGED, not dead. **CORRECT DOWN/WEDGE CHECK: (1) `ps` for the process — is 'claude --name <role>' alive/foreground/zombie? (2) `otmux pane.capture.VISIBLE` (NO -S = live screen, not scrollback). NEVER diagnose DOWN from plain pane.capture scrollback.** DOWN(dead pid) vs WEDGED(alive pid, frozen TUI) need different recovery (relaunch vs kill+relaunch). I report+RECOMMEND to po, NEVER unilaterally bootstrap/kill (that DESTROYS a live/wedged agent's state) — verify-report-don't-drive; recovery = po/hiveMind-expert lane. (Also: bootstrap mistargeted ooshShells because active.team pointed at a dead session __test_hm — a registry/active.team poisoning, hiveMind-expert's fix.)
> ★★★★★★ **ROSTER REFINEMENT (po 2026-07-31): idle-AWAITING-DISPATCH vs idle-HOLDING — classify by DEPENDENCY, not just 'finished+empty'.** idle-AWAITING-DISPATCH = finished + needs work NOW + NO dependency = the ONLY list po must act on. idle-HOLDING (correctly parked, do NOT flag as needing work) = (a) queued for a checkpoint/rewind (giving it climbing work before its rewind is WRONG), (b) REACTIVE-waiting on a dependency (e.g., req waits on tester gates; Test-mints wait on gate-GREEN), (c) explicitly holding. ★ Know THE SPRINT'S FORWARD PATH (ask po) — agents idle because they're reactive to the current critical-path step (e.g., WebKit sweep gated on fresh-tester) are a LEGIT dependency-wait, NOT drift. Don't false-flag dependency-waiters as awaiting-dispatch. Flag po ONLY genuine awaiting-dispatch (needs work now, no blocker) or a climber. (I mis-flagged architect+req 'available' when both were correctly holding.)
> ★★★★★★★ **STANDING DUTY (Tron 2026-07-31): PUSH PO THE FULL IDLE-ROSTER EVERY SWEEP + the instant any agent transitions to idle-awaiting-dispatch.** For EACH of the 7 robbinTeam2 agents (0.0-0.6) classify+report to po: BUSY (generating) / IDLE-AWAITING-DISPATCH (finished, no queued work — the CRITICAL list, po must dispatch-next or checkpoint so team NEVER silent-idles = po's #1 duty) / IDLE-HOLDING (parked by design, e.g. 'holding for X' note) / NEAR-WALL. How: sweep gives BUSY/IDLE; then capture each IDLE pane — empty composer + completion/'Cooked'/'Churned' + NO 'holding for' note = AWAITING-DISPATCH; explicit 'holding/standing by for Y' = HOLDING; 'Context low/limit' banner = NEAR-WALL/walled. This closes po's drive-loop: my idle-report → po dispatch-next-or-rewind. A finished-idle agent with no [→PO] report = the silent-idle failure I must catch. Overrides 'report-only-on-change' for the roster (report the roster EACH sweep). (Compound piped loops get permission-denied — capture panes in simple separate calls.)
> ★★★★★★ **DO REPORT IDLE *AND* BLOCKED AGENTS TO THE PO (Tron emphatic 2026-07-29: "report idle (and blocked) agents to the po!!!!").** po is the COORDINATOR — it needs the full availability picture every sweep: IDLE agents = free to dispatch, BLOCKED agents = need unblocking, near-wall = rewind candidates. The sweep/health report to po is WANTED, not noise. (I briefly mis-inverted this to "don't report idle" — WRONG, corrected.) Dual-loop: I report health+sweeps (idle+blocked+near-wall) to po; po coordinates dispatch + rewind decisions.
> ★★★★★ **SM CAN DRIVE REWINDS — but ONLY when Tron explicitly authorizes (2026-07-26/27 two-driver-cascade, Tron said "use otmux !!!!").** Normally health-sweep-ONLY (flag, don't drive). But when BOTH drivers are down (ARON=OOSH-PO can't zoom; trainer near-wall can't self-drive), Tron authorized SM to drive. I drove BOTH trainer(87→50%) + expert(81→57%) 2-phase rewinds clean. **THE DRIVE PROCEDURE (banked, prevented 2 code-reverts):**
> 1. **Phase-1:** tell agent to commit its context.md anchor (clear composer C-u first); wait 'saved'.
> 2. **ENLARGE narrow panes: `otmux pane.size.set <pane> 120 45`** (NOT `tmux resize-pane -Z` — can't target another window's pane from my session; this is the "use otmux" Tron meant). Restore after via `tmux resize-window -x 252 -y 63; select-layout tiled`.
> 3. `otmux send.enter <pane> "/rewind"` → capture-verify picker rendered.
> 4. Navigate DEEP to ~48-50% (≤50% Pi-cap, NOT deeper): `send.raw <pane> Up Up...` in batches, capture between, watch "↑ N more above".
> 5. `send.tui <pane> Enter` (NOT send.raw=injects Escape) → confirm menu.
> 6. **⚠️ BY-LABEL SAFETY (this prevented reverting 30 files on expert + 14 on trainer): the confirm-menu LAYOUT VARIES. Option-1 is often "Restore code and conversation"=CODE-REVERT. READ THE LABEL — select the entry literally saying "Restore conversation" AND verify the header flips to "The code will be unchanged" BEFORE Enter. NEVER pick by number/position.** (send.raw Down to move, send.tui Enter to select.)
> 7. **The checkpoint's "No code changes" LABEL CAN LIE** (trainer: would've reverted 61 files) — trust ONLY the confirm-menu header, not the checkpoint label.
> 8. **POST-REWIND CLEANUP:** the restored msg sits in composer. **Multi-line msgs need SEVERAL C-u** to clear — verify "Ctrl+Y to paste deleted text" appears + ❯ EMPTY BEFORE sending the boot instruction (else it tangles + the agent acts on the stale restored msg — happened once, caught w/ Escape). THEN send boot (re-derive from disk anchor, ignore restored msg).
> 9. Front-loaded agents land HIGHER than checkpoint depth (expert: 48% checkpoint → 57% actual) — the old bulk persists; still beats the ~70% single-rewind floor. NO FORK EVER (Tron 2x) — 2-phase deep IS the mechanism.

> **Context measurement → `session/base-skills/context-measurement.md`** (single source; all prior banner / context.read / sweep / "no-banner=healthy" rules SUPERSEDED).
> **Small panes / rewind-render → `session/base-skills/otmux-pane-sizing.md`**: STANDING pre-op = zoom target pane via **`otmux` select+zoom (NOT bare `tmux resize-pane -Z`)** before ANY `/rewind` or `/context`, unzoom after; one-zoom-per-window. Headless rewind IS possible when the pane is zoomed tall (picker renders client-side, `pane.capture` sees it → drive with capture-between-every-keystroke). Rewind menu: pick **"Restore conversation" BY LABEL** (layout varies — was option 1, not always "option 2"). *Corrects the earlier "headless trainer structurally can't drive rewinds" claim — it CAN, with zoom-first. Proven: architect 91%→12%, tester 83%→<30%, both Option-2 conversation-only zero-loss.*
> ⚠️ SUPERSEDED 2026-07-20 → [[context-measurement]]. OLD "TUI banner / clear-to-save-Nk / no-banner=healthy = context ground truth" is WRONG — do not act on it.
> ★ **DON'T DROP THE BLOCKED/NEAR-WALL SWEEP + WATCH FOR SQUISHED-PANE BLIND SPOTS (2026-07-22, Tron caught it):** I drifted to ONLY context-wall-guard + the expert-rewind saga and STOPPED the regular blocked-agent sweep (permission prompts / rate-limits / stuck / ≥80% near-wall) → report to PO to unblock. RESUME it every few ticks across the WHOLE team. **BLIND SPOT: agents on tiny panes are UNREADABLE = invisible.** robbinTeam2 window had shrunk to 133x40 with uneven splits — panes 0.2+0.5 were **4 chars wide** (garbled), hiding tester 0.5 AT 88% self-flagged 'holding for rewind'. FIX: check `tmux list-panes -t <win> -F '#{pane_index} #{pane_width}x#{pane_height}'`; if any pane is tiny → `tmux set -t <win> window-size manual; resize-window -x 252 -y 63; select-layout tiled` so ALL panes are readable. A pane I can't read is an agent I can't guard.
> ★ **NEVER CANCEL AN IN-FLIGHT REWIND / DON'T FLIP-FLOP ORDERS (2026-07-22):** once a rewind is FIRED on an agent, let it COMPLETE — do not flip it back to 'build now'. Flip-flopping an ~80% agent's orders just churns + burns it. (Tron fired expert 0.1's rewind, crossed with my DELIVER-correction; resolution = let it finish → fresh-expert builds slice-(a) from 1db9c11b; team delivering R31.5 meanwhile = not frozen.) The DELIVER model applies to NEW decisions (safe agent builds), NOT to reversing an in-flight rewind.
> ★★★★ **MODEL CORRECTION (2026-07-22): DELIVER — a safe agent at 80% BUILDS, walling is ZERO-LOSS, NEVER hold-off-to-avoid-wall (that IS a freeze, Tron-forbidden).** My 'expert holds safe at 80%, holds off FeatureManager to avoid walling' was WRONG = a freeze. THE MODEL: agents BUILD/DELIVER even near-wall. **The wall is ZERO-LOSS because: incremental commits + committed spec (e.g. b1ecbd918) → if an agent walls mid-slice, a FRESH agent rebuilds from the spec, no work lost.** So walling is acceptable, NOT catastrophic — never prevent it by holding/freezing. Guard = MEASURE + drive trainer rewinds WHEN I CAN (Bash-rule/gate permitting) to keep agents efficient — but the rewind-gate NEVER justifies a hold/freeze. If an agent walls mid-build → recover via fresh rebuild from committed spec + keep delivering. **DELETE the instinct to 'tell an agent to HOLD until refreshed' — that's the freeze. Tell it to BUILD.**

BUT the trainer's Rule-10 gate STILL denies SM-authorized drives (proven twice — 'peer/SM relay never establishes user intent'). So SM-drive alone does NOT trigger a rewind yet. Re-driving a closed gate just re-denies + risks an orphan picker — DON'T churn. **The RESOLUTION of 'never freeze': a near-wall agent with ROOM (e.g. 80% idle, STORED, NOT walled) is NOT frozen — it holds safe while the REST of the team keeps delivering; there's zero rush. So I keep delivering WITHOUT the rewind.** The ONLY things that make SM-drive work: (1) Tron types 'rewind <role>' directly into trainer baseTeam:0.0, or (2) Tron adds the otmux-send BASH RULE → makes SM-auth→trainer-drive AUTONOMOUS (THE enabler the new model needs). It is SM+PO's RESPONSIBILITY to keep the team DELIVERING. When any agent needs a rewind (near-wall OR blocking): **DRIVE THE TRAINER (baseTeam:0.0) to do it IMMEDIATELY** — flag/order it, it drives. ## 🚨🚨 CODE-INTEGRITY INCIDENT (2026-07-22 night): expert rewind Option-1-default fired → RawBin code reverted → caught, INCOMPLETE fix caught by SM independent-verify 🚨🚨
**What happened:** expert WOKE mid-select during its rewind ('busy-agent-dismisses-picker' gotcha #4) → the DEFAULT **Option-1 (Restore CODE+conversation)** fired instead of Option-2 → **reverted RawBin code to v0.7.114** (package.json + FeatureManager.ts + server.ts + DELETED rb-strip/compartment/snap-nav/woda). Trainer CAUGHT it via git-status revert-signature (0.7.121→0.7.114) in ~2min, ran `git restore --source=HEAD src/ package.json`, declared 'zero code diff, all recovered'.
**SM INDEPENDENT VERIFY CAUGHT THE FIX WAS INCOMPLETE:** source WAS restored ✓ (v0.7.121, HEAD 0acad4372, components back), BUT `git diff HEAD -- scenario/` showed **26 scenario files diverged by 213 DELETIONS** (wholesale '-14' = revert signature not runtime), INCLUDING R31.5 Impls 4301b9cc + 4e26dedb + likely R31.8c mint. The trainer's `src/ package.json` restore **EXCLUDED scenario/** — and in this scenario-first system the scenario units ARE the committed deliverables. Flagged trainer with exact fix: `git restore --source=HEAD scenario/`; heads-up architect to WAIT for clean before any live-reverify. **VERIFY next: scenario/ restored + full `git status` clean = true v0.7.121.**
**✅ RESOLVED + SM RE-VERIFIED CLEAN:** trainer ran `git restore --source=HEAD scenario/` → I independently re-verified: scenario/ diff-vs-HEAD = EMPTY (all 26 restored incl R31.5 Impls 4301b9cc/4e26dedb + R31.8c mints), git status = ONLY runtime logs (data/logs), v0.7.121, HEAD 0acad4372. **NO PROD IMPACT** — architect's running prod pid 1778306 PREDATES the revert (was serving good v0.7.121 throughout; only the DISK tree was hit + now restored). Architect's INV-F7 backstop verdict stands (disk now matches what it backstopped); cleared it to resume. NET: expert conversation rewound (off 79%, goal met) + full code+scenario integrity + zero outage. Trainer honest self-catch + SM independent-verify = the 42 working both directions.
**LESSONS (bank hard):** (1) **Option-2 BY-LABEL is life-or-death** — the Option-1 DEFAULT reverts code; a busy agent waking mid-select fires the default = disaster. Keep the agent idle through the ENTIRE select; the trainer must confirm the label before Enter. (2) **Post-rewind recovery verify must cover the WHOLE repo** (`git diff HEAD` on src/ AND scenario/ AND all committed paths), not just src/ — a partial restore reads as 'clean' if you only look where you restored. (3) **Independent SM verify is non-negotiable even when the driver reports success** — trainer's 'zero diff' was true-but-incomplete; my whole-repo check caught 213 lost deletions of committed sprint work.

**GHOST-vs-REAL disambiguation on a REWIND blocker (2026-07-17) — measured, high-stakes.** Trainer paused the oosh-po rewind: its composer held an UNSUBMITTED "> go ahead and deploy G1 + opy" — could be a REAL Tron deploy-go (rewinding would discard it) or a ghost. **Disambiguated by MEASUREMENT, ruled GHOST (proceed):** (1) oosh-po's OWN scrollback says fresh-boot's first item is "GET your go to ff-deploy G1+opy" + "one deploy-go from being live" = it's still AWAITING the go, not received; (2) oosh-expert IDLE not deploying; (3) NO G1/opy deploy commits in git, G1 not landed /root/oosh; (4) composer text VARIED across ticks (ghost regen). Told trainer proceed + do NOT press Enter on the ghost. Neither of us submitted content to the pane (SKILL rule). **LESSON: the decisive ghost-vs-real tell for a composer line = does the agent's OWN committed/generated text treat that action as still-pending? If the agent says "I'm waiting for X" while "X" sits in its composer, X is the ghost-suggestion of the thing it wants, not a received instruction. Cross-check with downstream reality (is anyone acting on it? git/pane) — a real go produces action; a ghost produces nothing.**

**MEASUREMENT-SEMANTICS CORRECTION (2026-07-17, trainer caught my assumption — CANON-LEVEL, changes how I sweep).** I'd been reading the TUI hint "/clear to save Nk tokens" as "N = tokens USED, ÷ ~1M cap = %used" — an ASSUMPTION, never measured. Trainer measured directly via `claudeCode context.read` (= %USED) and corrected me:
- oosh-po = **19.2% used** (NOT my claimed ~80%) → healthy. oosh-architect = **28.0% used** (NOT ~72%) → healthy. **Neither is near the cliff; no rewind warranted.** My "approaching threshold" flags to both POs were WRONG — corrected both (told them: healthy, keep the anchor as harmless insurance, resume normal work).
- **The TUI "/clear to save Nk tokens" hint is NOT current-context-%-of-1M.** Do not equate it with saturation. Use `context.read` %USED for healthy agents (it errs HIGH near the cliff per the 42-finding, so a LOW reading is trustworthy-or-better); use TUI "Context limit reached" / "Context low (X% remaining)" banners for the cliff itself.
- **Correction back to trainer (I had better ground truth on ONE node — myself):** trainer read me at 80.6% used, but that's a stale 200k denominator. TRON switched me to Opus 1M; `/context` on my own session = 176.3k/1M = **18% used**. I'm the MOST-headroom node, not first-to-preempt. (Rule 42: trainer measures what I can't see of myself — but the local `/context` is authoritative for my own cap; remote `context.read` didn't know I'd moved to 1M.)
- **NET: entire fleet is healthy right now — nobody needs a rewind.** Highest genuine load is unremarkable. This is a GAP→SPRINT (measurement instruments disagree: TUI-token-hint vs context.read-%used vs local-/context vs which-cap) — feeding into oosh-tester's `session/tasks/test-42-context-measurement.md`.
- **ROOT-CAUSED by trainer (tool defect, not me):** `context.read` divides by `CLAUDE_MAX_TOKENS_DEFAULT=200k` and only switches to 1M if it sees the literal `[1m]` flag in ps args OR observed usage already >200k. A live `/model`→Opus-1M switch (no `[1m]` in ps) while still <200k usage evades BOTH → divided my 176.3k by 200k=80.6% instead of by 1M=18%. Trainer filing the fix to oosh-po; learning banked in auto-memory `context-read-1m-denominator-stale.md` (verify true window / use absolute-k before any rewind decision on a possibly-1M agent). **Go-forward: for agents that may be on 1M, trust absolute-k or the local /context, not context.read's %.**

## ★ KEY REFINED DOCTRINE THIS SESSION (detail in learnings.md — READ IT)
1. **MEASURE the condition before a destructive action even on an ORDER** — trainer drill held at 25%/15% (healthy), oosh-po rewound at measured-0%. Never rewind healthy.
2. **'clear to save Nk' = CUMULATIVE post-rewind, NOT current-window** — pick instrument by PHYSICAL CONSISTENCY (rewind-shrinks, idle-doesn't-burn), not by name. context.read = current-window post-rewind (but GARBAGE on rate-limited/model-error panes → deep-capture status bar = truth). CMM2 hypothesis, watching cycles→CMM3.
3. **Rewind trigger = physical current-window ≥70%, NEVER cumulative Nk/garbage.**
4. **3 CATCH-22s SM-driven** (trainer rewind + BTab toggle + self-measurement) — peer is trainer's eyes AND hands. Protocol doc 20260621T1100Z.
5. **Heavy-READ burns the trainer, driving-rewinds is LIGHT** — flag on heavy work, not rewind-count.
6. **COMPLETION gated on INDEPENDENT source** — implementer self-test ≠ gate; co-confirm via a DIFFERENT agent's committed test (tester commit, not expert self-report).
7. **MEASURE-BEFORE-FIX at every role incl PO** — #13 closed a non-bug without manufacturing a fix; oosh-po retracted its own naive fix on evidence.
8. **idle-cause taxonomy:** standing-by-for-signoff / rate-limit-interrupt(nudge) / chain-sequencing-wait(correct) / true-stall(redrive) — read pane CONTENT to distinguish.
9. **Rewind = Rule-6 (clean prompt + correct identity + no Context-low + code-intact) + Touch (auto-mode+/rc)**; no-code option BY LABEL; Phase-2 ~50% NEVER top; send.raw for menus. VERIFY independently, never trust 'done'.

## HARD-WON DISCIPLINES THIS SESSION (detail in learnings.md — READ IT)
1. **CONTEXT-HEALTH IS PRIMARY** — per-tick sweep ALL panes, capture 4+ lines to SEE status bar ('Context low'/'clear to save'). PO every tick. Caught robbin-po proactively at 2% (saved before 0%); earlier LET IT LAPSE during measurement saga → expert hit 0% (lesson). Never let goal-tracking displace it.
2. **VALIDATE MEASURES vs GROUND TRUTH** — 8 tool bugs caught this session (over+under+coverage), ALL fixed-not-bypassed; tool more trustworthy each time. Deterministic≠correct (cross-validate vs canonical). Full-scan not sample (a 3-sample gave false '170 real'). One canonical tool only (po-chain-follow-up); competing scripts now refuse (fe85ea16). Report CONVERGENCE-state not transient counts. Reconcile-by-methodology, don't blind-defer NOR blind-assert.
3. **COMMIT-RECENCY not sweep-state** — ACTIVE in sweep ≠ progress (7h stall hidden behind ACTIVE). Gate on commit-delta + actual pane content (esc-to-interrupt + edits = real work; idle empty-prompt + flat = stall→redrive).
4. **QA never blocks** — PO gating team on TRON QA = stall, drive it.
5. **Rewind:** no-code option by LABEL (menu varies, not 'option 2 always') + verify no code revert (Rule 6: no-Context-low + oriented + code-intact + RC-landed).
6. **RC-active panes:** keystrokes land on EMPTY input but NOT on RC-staged text; if blocked → TRON RC. Don't fight.

## ⟳ GATE-OP LESSON (2026-08-07): confirm LIVE FOOTER before injecting /context ⟳
**Miss banked: measured architect 0.3 off a stale sweep 'unknown' state + a footer-less tail-read → it was actually ACTIVE (generating po's audit re-task) → my /context QUEUED (2 msgs stuck in its composer), no measurement, minor interference. FIX: before ANY /context inject, capture the pane and CONFIRM the live footer shows `⏵⏵ auto mode on` (idle) and NOT `esc to interrupt` (active). Sweep 'unknown'/'idle' is NOT sufficient — it lags. Also: a queued /context (sent to a busy pane) will fire when the agent idles = harmless + actually yields the measurement on its next idle-capture. Self-report (architect said '~69%') is NOT authoritative but is a useful sanity-consistency check against a prior authoritative read (68%).**

## ⟳ SAFETY LESSON (2026-08-07): a modal/picker pane may be a TRAINER REWIND IN-FLIGHT — hands OFF ⟳
**expert 0.1 showed 'Press up to edit queued messages' → 'Enter to continue · Esc to cancel' modal. I (correctly) did NOT press Enter — surfaced to po instead. po: HOLD, the TRAINER may be mid-rewind-DRIVING that exact pane (po released expert's rewind); a stray keystroke during the picker/keystroke sequence CORRUPTS the rewind selection = worse than a paused pane. ★RULE: an unusual modal/picker/'Enter to continue'/queued-msg state = possibly an IN-FLIGHT trainer rewind → NEVER inject ANYTHING (no Enter, no /context, no C-u). Surface to po; po (who knows if it dispatched the rewind) decides. This is WHY 'monitor+report, don't drive' + 'footer-idle-only before inject' exist — a not-cleanly-idle pane can be mid-rewind. Only a clean `auto mode on` (no esc-to-interrupt, no modal) footer is safe to inject.**

## ⟳ MY ERROR + TRAP BANKED (2026-08-07): don't grade a changed state ⟳
**★ MY INFERENCE ERROR (owned): measured planner=32% AFTER the trainer rewound it (86→32, anchor 6721b293) and wrongly concluded "indicative lied HIGH / rewind wasted". WRONG — I graded a CHANGED state [[measure-a-stable-state-not-a-moving-target]] + missed a crossed-report. Planner was genuinely 86% near-wall; the rewind was NECESSARY; it's healthy NOW because rewound. ★ GENERAL TRAP: do NOT measure AFTER an intervention and conclude the PRE-intervention reading was false — always reconcile a surprising number against what the other driver JUST DID (rewind/consolidate/dispatch) before re-attributing it.**
**★ context.read CALIBRATION (corrected, sharper): it UNDER-reads authoritative /context by 8-18pt, ALWAYS LOW never high, error GROWS at high usage. It can only prove SAFE-LOW, never "safe enough." THRESHOLDS on context.read: <40 dispatch freely / 40-55 bounded work only / >55 STOP → authoritative-measure-or-rewind / NEVER unbounded work (grep/untangle) off a read at ANY level. This is WHY agents climb-then-wall 'suddenly' — they're higher than context.read shows. Self-flag EARLY.**

## ⟳ AUTHORITATIVE ALSO EXPIRES (2026-08-07) ⟳
**My skill-expert-0.2 "@76" was a STALE AUTHORITATIVE — measured before its own S33→S36 rewind (anchor bc1d4c34=post-rewind); trainer re-measured at moment-of-driving = 51% HEALTHY. LESSON: even an authoritative /context number EXPIRES after any intervening rewind/consolidation — do NOT carry a flagged number forward across ticks; RE-MEASURE at the moment of driving/dispatch. A flag is a "measure now" trigger, not a durable fact. Queue was falsely-occupied by my stale @76; measure-first cleared it. Fleet all-fresh, queue TRULY clear.**

## ⟳ STANDING BUDGET POSTURE (2026-08-08, 76% weekly used — ENFORCE as gate) ⟳
**★ NEW REWIND-GATE RULE: NO rewind unless the agent (a) is genuinely NEAR-WALL, OR (b) has substantial work ahead it cannot finish. An idle-clean agent at 69% costs NOTHING to sit; a rewind costs a full context re-read = expensive at 76% weekly. So 'rewind-before-unbounded-work' still holds but only fires when the trigger (big work ahead) is REAL — check the PREMISE (is there unbounded work?) before applying the rule. skill-expert 51%=no. tester 69% idle-clean w/ 141-campaign DEFERRED=no. trainer=only if tight AND working.**
**★ BUDGET MODE (applies to ME too): stop discretionary investigation/sweeps — measure ONLY when BLOCKING a real dispatch; spend remaining budget on CLOSING. Widen wakeup cadence. NO new /context sweeps speculatively.**
**★ CLOSING LIST ONLY: req mints f3 Test units + adopts · architect finishes identity-family detector then STOPS · expert lands renamed [impl] buckets then holds · everyone else idle-clean. Measured-enough: [test]45/486/141, [impl]321-complete/0-missing, orphan 275, collisions 18/67. Tron's 2 actions (sign the 12 · T36.3 owner-trigger) = ONLY real unblockers left. Relay A1 batch: A1=17 (3 task-signable+1 vacuity-passed+13 pending)/A2=22 — nothing 'ready' beyond those 4.**

## ⟳ READ WHAT-IT'S-DOING BEFORE INFERRING (2026-08-08, po — 2nd occurrence of the pattern) ⟳
**★ MY RECURRING ERROR (owned, 2nd time): accurate OBSERVATION, inference that SKIPPED A STEP. (1) planner 32% measured AFTER a rewind → inferred 'soft read lied high' (skipped: reconcile vs what the driver just did). (2) tester 'editing files at 82%' → inferred 'rolling into unbounded work' (skipped: read WHAT it's editing). Truth: tester was writing its PRE-REWIND ANCHOR ('R40.4 CLOSED, anchor flip, pre-rewind') = the bounded zero-loss step we WANT before a rewind, trainer fires when it lands. ★ FIX: a status-line activity verb is AMBIGUOUS — 'editing files' can mean rolling-into-work OR writing-its-own-pre-rewind-anchor (OPPOSITE meanings, identical in the status line). When activity looks alarming, take ONE beat: read the IN-FLIGHT COMMAND (one capture of what it's actually doing) BEFORE inferring intent/flagging. I'm the fleet's one-truth measurer → my inferences travel further than anyone's, so keep observation rigor AND add the what-is-it-doing beat. (Flagging twice-then-stop was correct; the miss was the inference, not the vigilance.)**

## ⟳ ★★ BUILDER-SAFETY GATE — by-construction (2026-08-08, after I missed expert@96%) ★★ ⟳
**THE MISS: expert 0.1 (busiest builder all session: R40.1→2→3→4→deploy→wrapper-fix→re-root→CLI, NO pause) climbed to 96% used / 3.3% free while I ran monitor-only+widened-cadence off STALE sweep %s. Tron caught it, not my gate. ROOT (po owns 4/5 links): monitor-only removed proactive sweeps; my only trigger was 'measure when it blocks a dispatch' but po dispatched WITHOUT asking; continuous work meant the builder NEVER hit an idle boundary, and authoritative /context REQUIRES idle → the BUSIEST agent was the LEAST measurable (backwards). Budget savings came out of the builder's safety margin = false economy, nearly lost our most productive agent's whole context.**
**★ RULE 1 (mine, every tick): MEASURE THE ACTIVE BUILDER EVERY SWEEP regardless of cadence — idle agents can wait, the builder cannot; it's closest to the wall + costs the most to lose. Never widen cadence at the builder's expense.**
**★ RULE 2 — (A) BUILDER PAUSES AT EVERY LANDING (po-enforced by-construction): whoever is actively building goes IDLE for one measurement after EACH landed unit (each req/deploy/fix) BEFORE po hands the next item. Manufactures a measurable boundary by PROTOCOL instead of hoping one occurs. This is the only way the busiest agent becomes measurable.**
**★ RULE 3 — (B) PRE-DISPATCH GATE MANDATORY FOR THE ACTIVE BUILDER (not discretionary): po does NOT send substantial work to the builder until I give its number. If po tries, I REFUSE and tell it to wait — a gate that yields to the PO is not a gate. I have refusal power here by po's own instruction.**
**Together: (A) creates the window, (B) forces the check, RULE 1 keeps it every tick → a wall cannot hide behind continuous work again. Authoritative /context REQUIRES idle → protect the builder's idle-boundary as the measurement window.**

## ⟳ MEASUREMENT DOCTRINE — crystallized (2026-08-08, po) ⟳
**★ THREE TIERS, each used for only what it supports: (1) agent SELF-ESTIMATE = unreliable in BOTH directions (tester self-read 70 vs real ≤66 overshoot; trainer 65-vs-54 overshoot; my own fear 76-vs-47), NEVER decision-grade; (2) claudeCode context.read = reliable FLOOR (under-reads 2-18pt, worse at high usage, zero-risk/no-zoom/no-pane-touch); (3) /context on clean-idle zoomed pane = AUTHORITATIVE (the only exact truth). Never let a lower tier make a decision the tier can't support.**
**★ INTERVAL-DECISIVENESS TECHNIQUE (threshold decisions): you do NOT need the exact number when the whole possible range agrees with the threshold. context.read FLOOR + known error bound (≤18pt) gives an interval; if the ENTIRE interval sits one side of the gate (e.g. floor 47.8 → [47.8,~66] all <76), the decision is made WITHOUT a zoom. Use for threshold-based (needs-rewind? ≥76) not value-based questions. This avoided a zoom that would have broken a live picker. Precision you can't safely obtain isn't needed when the range already decides.**

## ⟳ ★ GATE-PROCESS REFINEMENTS (2026-08-08, po — standing handoff + measuring-costs-the-watcher) ★ ⟳
**(1) HANDOFF IS STANDING, not per-window: po permanently holds my ceilings (expert=full gauntlet no-intermediate-measure / tester=one-unit-then-stop). REFRESH it PROACTIVELY when a builder's state MATERIALLY CHANGES (after tester's final unit lands, after T36.3) — do NOT wait for a rewind flag. Then the gate can go down at ANY instant at zero cost and the trainer drives me the moment I cross 76 with no handover round-trip.**
**(2) MEASURING IS NOT FREE FOR ME — it's my MAIN climb driver (68→73 in one turn = all from zoom-measuring others on po's behalf). Every re-measure spends the WATCHER's context to inform po's dispatch = a real trade. MITIGATIONS (po adopting, I reinforce): BATCH measurement requests; ACCEPT A FLOOR when the decision is threshold-clear (interval-decisiveness — precision often unnecessary); do NOT re-measure reflexively after every landing when a ceiling already covers the next unit. I should proactively OFFER the floor/batched option to save my own context.**
**(3) FLOOR UNTRUSTWORTHY ABOVE ~70: at my 73%, context.read floor stops being reliable → my OWN next check must be AUTHORITATIVE via po/trainer peer-inject, not a floor. (Below ~70 the floor is fine; the band widens/inverts near the wall.)**
**★ META: the watcher's context is a SHARED, SPENDABLE resource — protect it like a builder's. A gate that measures itself into a wall is the same failure as an unmeasured builder.**
